📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

狂犬病病毒本期文献呈现多方向研究进展

本期重点文献按发表日期、相关性、证据等级和研究质量综合排序，研究结果与综述证据分别核验后形成以下进展。

  • 在检查的 51 例疑似病例中,有 46 例(90.2%)经实验室确诊为狂犬病,其中家犬占提交病例的 96.1%,猫占 3.9%,在整个研究期间均发现了确诊病例。 [paper-963b1a18a14523fa]
  • 八项实地随机对照试验 (RCT) 表明,与对照相比,One Health 干预措施有效减少了疾病发生或病原体检测。该审查强调,一小部分但严格的研究提供了有效性的明确证据,而更广泛的评估因各研究对有效性的定义不一致而受到阻碍。 [paper-6cc8d7c282a20794]
  • XGBoost 模型在独立测试集上表现出卓越的预测性能,受试者工作特征曲线下面积为 0.881,而 SHAP 分析确保了临床可解释性。 [paper-cbd8d7810b72f83a]
  • 评论还揭示了对自由漫游狗的不同看法,从支持人道疫苗接种、绝育和共存到呼吁移除或扑杀。 [paper-78c12539ae601d62]
  • 系统发育分析揭示了与当代印度狂犬病病毒株的密切遗传相关性。 [paper-784af4221daac0f4]

本期研究方向以入选文献实际覆盖的临床、流行病学、宿主生态、诊断或分子监测主题为准。

证据提醒：部分证据来自摘要、观察性研究或叙述性综述，结论应结合研究设计和证据等级理解。

Recent Rabies virus literature

This literature brief prioritizes publications in the active reporting window and ranks them by relevance, evidence availability, study quality, recency and source convergence.

  • Of the 51 suspected cases examined, 46 (90.2%) were laboratory confirmed as rabies, with domestic dogs accounting for 96.1% of submissions and cats representing 3.9%, and confirmed cases were detected throughout the study period. [paper-963b1a18a14523fa]
  • Eight field-based randomized controlled trials (RCTs) demonstrated that One Health interventions effectively reduced disease occurrence or pathogen detection compared to controls. [paper-6cc8d7c282a20794]
  • The XGBoost model demonstrated superior predictive performance on the independent test set, with an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.881, while SHAP analysis ensured clinical interpretability. [paper-cbd8d7810b72f83a]
  • Comments also revealed contrasting views on free-roaming dogs, ranging from support for humane vaccination, sterilisation and coexistence to calls for removal or culling. [paper-78c12539ae601d62]
  • Phylogenetic analysis revealed close genetic relatedness to contemporary Indian rabies virus strains. [paper-784af4221daac0f4]

The direction of this week's research is defined by the clinical, epidemiological, ecological, diagnostic and molecular topics actually represented by the eligible publications.

Evidence caveat: Some evidence is abstract-only, observational or derived from narrative reviews; conclusions should be interpreted in light of study design and evidence strength.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

全球多地报告狂犬病病例,南卡罗来纳州野生动物检测呈阳性。

本周全球多地报告狂犬病确诊病例,包括美国南卡罗来纳州乔治敦县一只狐狸检测呈阳性,以及尼日利亚联邦首都区因疫苗短缺导致疫情爆发。世界动物卫生组织指出南部非洲海狗中存在持续传播,引发对狂犬病生态转变的担忧。

  • 南卡罗来纳州乔治敦县一只狐狸经公共卫生部门证实狂犬病检测呈阳性,当局敦促居民避免接触野生动物并确保宠物接种狂犬病疫苗。
  • 尼日利亚联邦首都区报告8例狂犬病确诊病例,其中2人死亡,疫情与2025年底至2026年初的疫苗短缺有关,政府正计划开展大规模疫苗接种。
  • 世界动物卫生组织报告南部非洲海狗中存在持续狂犬病传播,被描述为狂犬病生态学上的前所未有的转变,强调“同一个健康”方法的重要性。
  • 美国多地蝙蝠、浣熊等动物狂犬病检测呈阳性,包括圣地亚哥县第19只阳性蝙蝠、圣劳伦斯县第8只阳性浣熊,以及阿拉巴马州召回7批次疫苗。
  • 罗德岛州2026年狂犬病动物阳性数量已超过去五年平均水平,32人因接触狂犬病接受治疗,浣熊和蝙蝠为主要传播媒介。
  • 科罗拉多州兽医组织虚拟5K活动筹集近1.4万美元支持华盛顿州立大学在坦桑尼亚的狂犬病控制项目,旨在资助犬类疫苗接种。

当前狂犬病风险呈现全球性上升趋势,特别是在野生动物中。美国南部和东北部地区蝙蝠和浣熊病例显著增加,南卡罗来纳州和罗德岛州已超过历史平均水平。尼日利亚的疫情爆发凸显了疫苗供应不足对公共卫生的威胁。官方机构正加强监测、疫苗接种和公众教育,呼吁居民避免接触野生动物并及时寻求医疗评估。世界动物卫生组织对南部非洲海狗中持续传播的警告表明,狂犬病生态正在发生不可逆转的转变,需要跨部门协作应对。

证据提醒：部分报告缺乏详细数据,如特哈马县7月蝙蝠病例的具体数量、阿拉巴马州召回疫苗的具体影响范围,以及蒙大拿州和怀特斯伯勒病例的完整背景信息。部分来源翻译不完整或质量较低,可能影响信息的准确性。此外,部分事件(如圣地亚哥动物园蝙蝠)未披露人类或动物暴露情况,增加了风险评估的不确定性。

Global Rabies Cases Reported as South Carolina Wildlife Tests Positive.

Multiple regions reported rabies cases this week, including a confirmed positive fox in Georgetown County, South Carolina, and a rabies outbreak in Nigeria's Federal Capital Territory linked to vaccine shortages. The World Organisation for Animal Health highlighted sustained transmission in Cape fur seals in southern Africa, raising concerns about an unprecedented shift in rabies ecology.

  • A fox in Georgetown County, South Carolina, tested positive for rabies, confirmed by the state's Public Health Department, urging residents to avoid wildlife contact and ensure pet vaccinations.
  • The Federal Capital Territory in Nigeria reported eight rabies cases, including two fatalities, linked to vaccine shortages in late 2025 and early 2026, with the government planning mass vaccination.
  • The World Organisation for Animal Health reported sustained rabies transmission in Cape fur seals in southern Africa, described as an unprecedented shift in rabies ecology, emphasizing the One Health approach.
  • Multiple U.S. locations reported rabies-positive bats and raccoons, including the 19th bat in San Diego County and the eighth raccoon in St. Lawrence County, along with a recall of seven vaccine batches in Alabama.
  • Rhode Island's rabid animal cases in 2026 have exceeded the five-year average, with 32 humans exposed and treated, as raccoons and bats remain primary carriers.
  • A virtual 5K fundraiser in Colorado raised nearly $14,000 to support Washington State University's rabies control program in Tanzania, aiming to fund canine vaccinations.

Rabies risk is globally rising, particularly among wildlife. Cases in bats and raccoons have significantly increased in the southern and northeastern United States, with South Carolina and Rhode Island surpassing historical averages. The outbreak in Nigeria underscores the threat of vaccine shortages to public health. Authorities are strengthening surveillance, vaccination, and public education, urging residents to avoid wildlife contact and seek medical evaluation promptly. The World Organisation for Animal Health's warning about sustained transmission in Cape fur seals indicates an irreversible shift in rabies ecology, requiring cross-sector collaboration.

Evidence caveat: Several reports lack detailed data, such as the specific number of bats testing positive in Tehama County in July, the full impact scope of the vaccine recall in Alabama, and complete background information for cases in Montana and Whitesboro. Some sources have incomplete or low-quality translations, potentially affecting accuracy. Additionally, some events, such as the bat at the San Diego Zoo, did not disclose human or animal exposure, adding uncertainty to risk assessment.

基于机器学习的狂犬病暴露患者健康焦虑轨迹分析

Analysis of health anxiety trajectories in rabies exposure patients based on machine learning
作者： Yue’e Ma, Hua Xie, Xin Liu, Bin Lu, Minghe Jiang, Chenghang Mo, Hongbo Li, Fangzhu Li, Lan Liang, Guodong Xiong
摘要中文翻译目的:识别狂犬病初次暴露患者健康焦虑的动态演变轨迹,并利用机器学习算法对健康焦虑轨迹进行预测分析。方法:选取2024年1月至2025年10月期间南宁市某三级医院狂犬病暴露患者548例,按5剂次免疫方案,采用健康焦虑量表评估暴露后0、3、7、14、28天的健康焦虑水平。采用潜在变量增长混合模型来识别焦虑发展轨迹。利用6种机器学习算法建立高危健康焦虑识别模型,并对模型性能进行综合评价,并采用SHAP算法进行模型解释。结果:548 名患者全部完成了调查。潜变量增长混合模型确定了三种健康焦虑发展轨迹:低水平焦虑稳定组、中高水平焦虑改善组和高持续焦虑难以缓解组。多因素logistic回归分析显示,女性、大学本科及以上学历、狂犬病知识较高、合并症较多、III级暴露、延迟就医是“高持续性焦虑难以缓解组”的独立危险因素。相比之下,较高水平的健康素养被认为是一个保护因素。XGBoost 模型在独立测试集上表现出卓越的预测性能,受试者工作特征曲线下面积为 0.881,而 SHAP 分析确保了临床可解释性。XGBoost 模型的 SHAP 分析显示,健康素养、感知社会支持、狂犬病知识、支付方式和动物来源是预测“高持续性群体”的五个最重要特征。结论:狂犬病暴露患者的健康焦虑轨迹存在异质性。基于机器学习的预测模型有助于早期识别高危人群,为实施精准心理干预提供基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
识别初次暴露于狂犬病的患者健康焦虑的动态演变轨迹,并利用机器学习算法对健康焦虑轨迹进行预测分析。
设计与对象
选取2024年1月至2025年10月期间南宁市某三甲医院狂犬病暴露患者548例。
核心方法
利用6种机器学习算法建立高危健康焦虑识别模型,并对模型性能进行综合评价,并采用SHAP算法进行模型解释。
主要结果
XGBoost 模型在独立测试集上表现出卓越的预测性能,受试者工作特征曲线下面积为 0.881,而 SHAP 分析确保了临床可解释性。
解释与创新
XGBoost 模型的 SHAP 分析显示,健康素养、感知社会支持、狂犬病知识、支付方式和动物来源是预测“高持续性群体”的五个最重要特征。
科研与公卫意义
狂犬病暴露患者的健康焦虑轨迹存在异质性。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取基于检索到的全文证据。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Analysis of health anxiety trajectories in rabies exposure patients based on machine learning

Authors: Yue’e Ma, Hua Xie, Xin Liu, Bin Lu, Minghe Jiang, Chenghang Mo, Hongbo Li, Fangzhu Li, Lan Liang, Guodong Xiong
Original Abstract
OBJECTIVE: To identify the dynamic evolution trajectory of health anxiety in patients with initial rabies exposure and to conduct predictive analysis of health anxiety trajectories using machine learning algorithms. METHODS: A total of 548 rabies exposure patients from a tertiary hospital in Nanning were selected between January 2024 and October 2025. Health anxiety levels were assessed at 0, 3, 7, 14, and 28 days post-exposure using the Health Anxiety Scale following a 5-dose immunization schedule. A latent variable growth mixture model was employed to identify anxiety development trajectories. Six machine learning algorithms were utilized to establish a high-risk health anxiety identification model, and a comprehensive evaluation of the model's performance was conducted, with the SHAP algorithm used for model interpretation. RESULTS: All 548 patients completed the survey. The latent variable growth mixture model identified three health anxiety development trajectories: low-level anxiety-stable group, moderate to high-level anxiety-improving group, and high persistent anxiety-hard to relieve group. Multivariate logistic regression analysis indicated that being female, having a college degree or higher, high rabies knowledge, a greater number of comorbidities, level III exposure, and delayed medical treatment were independent risk factors belonging to the 'high persistent anxiety-hard to relieve group.' In contrast, a higher level of health literacy was identified as a protective factor. The XGBoost model demonstrated superior predictive performance on the independent test set, with an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.881, while SHAP analysis ensured clinical interpretability. SHAP analysis of the XGBoost model revealed that health literacy, Perceived social support, rabies knowledge, Payment method and Animal source were the five most important features predicting the 'high persistent group. CONCLUSION: There is heterogeneity in the health anxiety trajectories of patients exposed to rabies. A predictive model based on machine learning can facilitate the early identification of high-risk individuals, providing a basis for implementing precise psychological interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
To identify the dynamic evolution trajectory of health anxiety in patients with initial rabies exposure and to conduct predictive analysis of health anxiety trajectories using machine learning algorithms.
Design and population
A total of 548 rabies exposure patients from a tertiary hospital in Nanning were selected between January 2024 and October 2025.
Core methods
Six machine learning algorithms were utilized to establish a high-risk health anxiety identification model, and a comprehensive evaluation of the model's performance was conducted, with the SHAP algorithm used for model interpretation.
Main results
The XGBoost model demonstrated superior predictive performance on the independent test set, with an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.881, while SHAP analysis ensured clinical interpretability.
Interpretation and novelty
SHAP analysis of the XGBoost model revealed that health literacy, Perceived social support, rabies knowledge, Payment method and Animal source were the five most important features predicting the 'high persistent group.
Scientific and public-health significance
There is heterogeneity in the health anxiety trajectories of patients exposed to rabies.
Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on retrieved fulltext evidence. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.

对狂犬病的恐惧和被咬后的心理压力:对印度 YouTube 评论的定性研究

Fear of rabies and post-bite psychological stress: a qualitative study of YouTube comments from India
作者： Harish Kumar Tiwari, Parimala Mohanty, Arindam Biswas, Jully Gogoi-Tiwari
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 狗介导的狂犬病仍然是一种致命但可预防的人畜共患疾病,并且在印度继续造成全球狂犬病死亡的很大一部分。尽管生物医学和流行病学方面已有详细记录,但对狂犬病暴露的心理影响以及公众对其的恐惧却很少关注。目的 探讨印度公共网络话语中对狂犬病的认知和情感体验,并描述被狗咬伤后表达的焦虑和痛苦模式。方法 从 6 个印度 YouTube 视频中检索公众评论 (n = 300),其中包含狂犬病相关内容,包括新闻辩论、医学解释、致命病例叙述、自由漫步的狗攻击镜头以及涉及暴露后预防 (PEP) 的短视频。在筛选相关性后,使用 Braun 和 Clarke 的六阶段归纳主题分析框架对 172 条评论进行了分析。结果 确定了六个主要主题:狂犬病死亡焦虑;医疗焦虑和寻求安慰;家庭保护和社会影响;污染恐惧和避免;高度警惕;以及对自由漫步的狗的有争议的态度。以恐惧为导向的主题占主导地位。死亡焦虑(26%)和寻求医疗保证(30%)是最常见的主题类别。许多评论描述了持续的沉思、反复的伤口检查、疫苗充足性的不确定性以及接触后很长时间内持续的痛苦。还描述了家庭层面的行为变化和回避做法。评论还揭示了对自由漫游狗的不同看法,从支持人道疫苗接种、绝育和共存到呼吁移除或扑杀。结论 经过分析的社交媒体言论表明,狂犬病不仅被视为生物医学威胁,而且也是持续不确定性和恐惧的根源。数字评论空间揭示了不确定性、对死亡的恐惧以及感知暴露后的行为适应。将心理考虑和明确的风险沟通纳入狂犬病控制计划可能会增强公众对预防和治疗策略的信心。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
目的 探讨印度公共网络话语中对狂犬病的认知和情感体验,并描述被狗咬伤后表达的焦虑和痛苦模式。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
方法 从 6 个印度 YouTube 视频中检索公众评论 (n = 300),其中包含狂犬病相关内容,包括新闻辩论、医学解释、致命病例叙述、自由漫步的狗攻击镜头以及涉及暴露后预防 (PEP) 的短视频。
主要结果
评论还揭示了对自由漫游狗的不同看法,从支持人道疫苗接种、绝育和共存到呼吁移除或扑杀。
解释与创新
结论 经过分析的社交媒体言论表明,狂犬病不仅被视为生物医学威胁,而且也是持续不确定性和恐惧的根源。
科研与公卫意义
将心理考虑和明确的风险沟通纳入狂犬病控制计划可能会增强公众对预防和治疗策略的信心。
局限与证据强度
数字评论空间揭示了不确定性、对死亡的恐惧以及感知暴露后的行为适应。

Fear of rabies and post-bite psychological stress: a qualitative study of YouTube comments from India

Authors: Harish Kumar Tiwari, Parimala Mohanty, Arindam Biswas, Jully Gogoi-Tiwari
Original Abstract
Abstract Background Dog-mediated rabies remains a fatal yet preventable zoonotic disease and continues to cause a substantial proportion of global rabies deaths in India. Although biomedical and epidemiological aspects are well documented, far less attention has been paid to the psychological impact of rabies exposure and the public fear surrounding it. Objective To explore how rabies is perceived and emotionally experienced in public online discourse in India, and to characterise patterns of anxiety and distress expressed following dog-bite exposure. Methods Public comments (n = 300) were retrieved from six Indian YouTube videos featuring rabies-related content, including news debates, medical explanations, fatal case narratives, free-roaming-dog attack footage, and short videos referencing post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP). Following screening for relevance, 172 comments were analysed using Braun and Clarke’s six-phase inductive thematic analysis framework. Results Six principal themes were identified: Rabies Death Anxiety; Medical Anxiety and Reassurance Seeking; Family Protection and Social Impact; Contamination Fear and Avoidance; Hypervigilance; and Contested Attitudes towards Free-Roaming Dogs. Fear-oriented themes predominated. Death anxiety (26%) and medical reassurance seeking (30%) were the most frequent thematic categories. Many comments described persistent rumination, repeated wound-checking, uncertainty regarding vaccine adequacy, and ongoing distress long after exposure. Family-level behavioural changes and avoidance practices were also described. Comments also revealed contrasting views on free-roaming dogs, ranging from support for humane vaccination, sterilisation and coexistence to calls for removal or culling. Conclusion The analysed social media discourse suggests that rabies is represented not only as a biomedical threat, but also as a source of persistent uncertainty and fear. Digital comment spaces reveal uncertainty, fear of fatality, and behavioural adaptation following perceived exposure. Integrating psychological considerations and clear risk communication into rabies-control programmes may strengthen public confidence in prevention and treatment strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Objective To explore how rabies is perceived and emotionally experienced in public online discourse in India, and to characterise patterns of anxiety and distress expressed following dog-bite exposure.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.
Core methods
Methods Public comments (n = 300) were retrieved from six Indian YouTube videos featuring rabies-related content, including news debates, medical explanations, fatal case narratives, free-roaming-dog attack footage, and short videos referencing post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP).
Main results
Comments also revealed contrasting views on free-roaming dogs, ranging from support for humane vaccination, sterilisation and coexistence to calls for removal or culling.
Interpretation and novelty
Conclusion The analysed social media discourse suggests that rabies is represented not only as a biomedical threat, but also as a source of persistent uncertainty and fear.
Scientific and public-health significance
Integrating psychological considerations and clear risk communication into rabies-control programmes may strengthen public confidence in prevention and treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
Digital comment spaces reveal uncertainty, fear of fatality, and behavioural adaptation following perceived exposure.

来自一只 54 天大、表现不典型的小狗的类似北极 1a 狂犬病病毒的特征,印度浦那,2026 年

Characterization of an Arctic-like 1a rabies virus from a 54-day-old puppy with atypical presentation, Pune, India, 2026
作者： Padinjaremattathil Thankappan Ullas, Vikas Sharma, Veena Vipat, Shweta Choudhary, Alfia Fathima Ashraf, Ranjana Mariyam Raju, Venu Kotturi, Kunal Sudhakar Sakhare, Vijay Parashramji Bondre, Shweta Choudhari
摘要中文翻译摘要: 在印度,狂犬病仍然是一种严重未报告的致命人畜共患疾病,该国狗群中以类北极 1a (AL1a) 谱系为主。虽然狗的非典型临床表现可能会延误诊断并增加人类接触风险,但新生犬狂犬病的基因组和临床数据仍然有限。这项研究报告了一只 54 天大的未接种疫苗的德国牧羊犬幼犬感染狂犬病的特殊病例,该幼犬出现严重瘙痒和自咬行为。由于临床反应不佳,这只小狗被安乐死。尸检显示脑组织中存在病毒抗原(通过直接荧光抗体测试)和病毒 RNA(通过实时 RTPCR)。全基因组测序恢复了近乎完整的狂犬病病毒基因组(11,947 个核苷酸;99.5% 基因组覆盖率),属于 AL1a_A1.1 亚系。系统发育分析揭示了与当代印度狂犬病病毒株的密切遗传相关性。比较基因组分析在磷蛋白、基质、糖蛋白和聚合酶基因中分别鉴定出 4、3、6 和 8 个非同义取代。该病例是有记录的最年轻的具有非典型表现的犬狂犬病病例之一,由 AL1a 病毒分支引起。我们的研究结果强调了与新生儿犬狂犬病相关的风险、对非典型病例加强临床怀疑的必要性,以及基因组监测对监测狂犬病流行地区不断演变的狂犬病病毒谱系的重要性。

Characterization of an Arctic-like 1a rabies virus from a 54-day-old puppy with atypical presentation, Pune, India, 2026

Authors: Padinjaremattathil Thankappan Ullas, Vikas Sharma, Veena Vipat, Shweta Choudhary, Alfia Fathima Ashraf, Ranjana Mariyam Raju, Venu Kotturi, Kunal Sudhakar Sakhare, Vijay Parashramji Bondre, Shweta Choudhari
Original Abstract
Abstract Rabies remains a significantly underreported fatal zoonosis in India, where the Arctic-like 1a (AL1a) lineage predominates in dog populations. While atypical clinical presentations in dogs can delay diagnosis and increase human exposure risk, genomic and clinical data on neonatal canine rabies remain limited. This study reports an exceptional case of rabies in a 54-day old unvaccinated German shepherd puppy which presented with severe pruritus and self-biting behaviour. The puppy was euthanized due to poor clinical response. Post-mortem testing revealed viral antigen (by Direct Fluorescent Antibody Test) and viral RNA (by real-time RTPCR) in the brain tissue. Whole-genome sequencing recovered a near-complete rabies virus genome (11,947 nucleotides; 99.5% genome coverage), classified within the AL1a_A1.1 sublineage. Phylogenetic analysis revealed close genetic relatedness to contemporary Indian rabies virus strains. Comparative genomic analysis identified 4, 3, 6, and 8 non-synonymous substitutions in the phosphoprotein, matrix, glycoprotein, and polymerase genes, respectively. This case is one of the youngest documented cases of canine rabies with atypical manifestations, caused by the AL1a viral clade. Our findings highlight the risks associated with neonatal canine rabies, the need for heightened clinical suspicion in atypical cases, and the importance of genomic surveillance to monitor evolving rabies virus lineages in endemic regions.

加纳博诺东部地区动物狂犬病实验室监测:六年回顾性研究(2020 – 2025 年)

Laboratory-Based Surveillance of Animal Rabies in the Bono East Region of Ghana: A Six-Year Retrospective Study (2020 – 2025)
作者： Prince Kyere Dwaah, Sylvia Afriyie Squire, Hellen Nketia Djang-Fordjour, Issah Bagulo, Anthony Osei-Tutu, Samuel Opokuware, Emmanuel Yaw Opoku, Roger Twum Gyarko, David Kando, Abraham Num
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 狗介导的狂犬病仍然是一种被忽视的主要热带疾病,也是撒哈拉以南非洲地区可预防的人类死亡的一个重要原因。尽管加纳正在进行控制工作,但实验室确认的监测数据有限,阻碍了对疾病负担的准确评估和基于证据的干预措施的发展。本研究调查了疑似动物提交的实验室确诊狂犬病病例的流行病学特征,并评估了常规诊断监测对狂犬病控制的贡献。方法/主要结果 使用 2020 年至 2025 年间提交给加纳地区兽医实验室的疑似狂犬病病例的实验室监测记录进行回顾性分析。使用塞勒染色、直接荧光抗体测试 (FAT) 和聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 对脑组织样本进行检查。提取流行病学信息并进行描述性分析,包括动物种类、疫苗接种史、所有权状况、咬伤史和诊断结果。在检查的 51 例疑似病例中,46 例(90.2%)经实验室确诊为狂犬病。家养狗占提交内容的 96.1%,而猫则占 3.9%。大多数可疑动物要么未接种疫苗(64.7%),要么疫苗接种状况未知(33.3%),据报道,90.2%的动物在提交前曾咬伤过人类。在整个研究期间发现了确诊病例,表明研究区域内持续存在病毒传播和持续的人畜共患风险。结论/意义 实验室确诊的狂犬病病例比例持续较高,表明狂犬病病毒在家畜中持续传播,并凸显了对人类健康的重大风险。传统和先进实验室诊断方法的结合加强了监测并改善了病例确认。这些发现强调迫切需要扩大狗的疫苗接种覆盖范围、加强基于实验室的监测、改进咬伤报告系统以及加强兽医和公共卫生部门之间的合作。加强综合“同一个健康”战略对于加快实现到 2030 年消除由狗传播的人类狂犬病这一全球目标至关重要。作者摘要 狂犬病仍然是世界上最古老、最具破坏性的人畜共患疾病之一,这种疾病致命但可以预防,每年导致数千人死亡,特别是在非洲和亚洲。在加纳,家犬是狂犬病病毒的主要宿主,但许多地区的常规实验室监测提供的动物狂犬病流行病学信息有限。我们回顾了波诺东地区泰奇曼地区兽医实验室六年(2020 年至 2025 年)的实验室记录,以描述疑似动物狂犬病病例和实验室确诊感染的流行病学特征。在提交诊断的 51 份脑样本中,46 份(90.2%)被证实狂犬病呈阳性。家犬几乎占所有提交的病例,大多数确诊病例涉及未接种疫苗或疫苗接种史未知的动物。此外,据报道,大多数可疑动物在实验室调查之前就曾咬伤过人类,这凸显了犬类狂犬病对公共健康造成的持续风险。该研究还记录了如何通过与国家参考实验室合作加强区域诊断能力,从而能够常规使用国际推荐的诊断方法,例如荧光抗体测试和聚合酶链反应。这些发现为支持加强狂犬病监测、增加大规模狗疫苗接种、提高公众意识和加强“同一个健康”合作提供了宝贵的证据。这些措施对于减少人类狂犬病死亡和支持加纳为到 2030 年消除由狗传播的人类狂犬病全球目标做出贡献至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狗介导的狂犬病仍然是一种被忽视的主要热带疾病,也是撒哈拉以南非洲地区可预防人类死亡的重要原因,但实验室确认的监测数据有限,阻碍了加纳疾病负担的准确评估和循证干预措施的制定。
设计与对象
使用 2020 年至 2025 年间提交给加纳地区兽医实验室的疑似狂犬病病例的实验室监测记录进行回顾性分析,检查了 51 例疑似病例的脑组织样本。
核心方法
使用卖方染色、直接荧光抗体测试(FAT)和聚合酶链反应(PCR)检查脑组织样本,同时提取包括动物种类、疫苗接种史、所有权状况、咬伤史和诊断结果在内的流行病学信息并进行描述性分析。
主要结果
在检查的 51 例疑似病例中,有 46 例(90.2%)经实验室确诊为狂犬病,其中家犬占提交病例的 96.1%,猫占 3.9%,在整个研究期间均发现了确诊病例。
解释与创新
实验室确诊的狂犬病病例比例持续较高,表明狂犬病病毒在家畜中持续传播,凸显了对人类健康的重大风险,而传统和先进实验室诊断方法的结合加强了监测并改善了病例确认。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调,迫切需要扩大狗的疫苗接种覆盖范围,加强基于实验室的监测,改进咬伤报告系统,并加强兽医和公共卫生部门之间的合作,以加快实现到 2030 年消除狗介导的人类狂犬病的全球目标。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据没有报告具体的局限性,但回顾性设计和对实验室记录的依赖可能会引入选择偏差并限制对其他地区的普遍适用性。

Laboratory-Based Surveillance of Animal Rabies in the Bono East Region of Ghana: A Six-Year Retrospective Study (2020 – 2025)

Authors: Prince Kyere Dwaah, Sylvia Afriyie Squire, Hellen Nketia Djang-Fordjour, Issah Bagulo, Anthony Osei-Tutu, Samuel Opokuware, Emmanuel Yaw Opoku, Roger Twum Gyarko, David Kando, Abraham Num
Original Abstract
Abstract Background Dog-mediated rabies remains a major neglected tropical disease and an important cause of preventable human mortality in sub-Saharan Africa. Despite ongoing control efforts, limited laboratory-confirmed surveillance data hinder accurate assessment of the disease burden and the development of evidence-based interventions in Ghana. This study investigated the epidemiological characteristics of laboratory-confirmed rabies cases among suspected animal submissions and evaluated the contribution of routine diagnostic surveillance to rabies control. Methodology/Principal Findings A retrospective analysis was conducted using laboratory surveillance records of suspected rabies cases submitted to a regional veterinary laboratory in Ghana between 2020 and 2025. Brain tissue samples were examined using Seller’s staining, the direct fluorescent antibody test (FAT), and polymerase chain reaction (PCR). Epidemiological information, including animal species, vaccination history, ownership status, bite history, and diagnostic outcomes, was extracted and analysed descriptively. Of the 51 suspected cases examined, 46 (90.2%) were laboratory confirmed as rabies. Domestic dogs accounted for 96.1% of submissions, while cats represented 3.9%. Most suspected animals were either unvaccinated (64.7%) or had unknown vaccination status (33.3%), and 90.2% had reportedly bitten humans before submission. Confirmed cases were detected throughout the study period, indicating sustained viral circulation and continued zoonotic risk within the study area. Conclusions/Significance The consistently high proportion of laboratory-confirmed rabies cases demonstrates persistent transmission of rabies virus among domestic animals and highlights substantial risks to human health. The integration of conventional and advanced laboratory diagnostic methods strengthened surveillance and improved case confirmation. These findings underscore the urgent need to expand dog vaccination coverage, strengthen laboratory-based surveillance, improve bite reporting systems, and enhance collaboration between veterinary and public health sectors. Reinforcing integrated One Health strategies will be essential to accelerate progress towards the global goal of eliminating dog-mediated human rabies by 2030. Author Summary Rabies remains one of the world’s oldest and most devastating zoonotic diseases, which is fatal yet preventable, causing thousands of human deaths each year, particularly in Africa and Asia. In Ghana, domestic dogs are the principal reservoir of the rabies virus, but information on the epidemiology of animal rabies from routine laboratory surveillance is limited in many regions. We reviewed six years (2020 – 2025) of laboratory records from the Regional Veterinary Laboratory in Techiman, Bono East Region, to describe the epidemiological characteristics of suspected animal rabies cases and laboratory-confirmed infections. Of the 51 brain samples submitted for diagnosis, 46 (90.2%) were confirmed positive for rabies. Domestic dogs accounted for almost all submissions, and most confirmed cases involved animals that were either unvaccinated or had an unknown vaccination history. Furthermore, most suspected animals had reportedly bitten humans before laboratory investigation, highlighting the continuing public health risk posed by canine rabies. The study also documents how regional diagnostic capacity was strengthened through collaboration with national reference laboratories, enabling the routine use of internationally recommended diagnostic methods such as the fluorescent antibody test and polymerase chain reaction. These findings provide valuable evidence to support enhanced rabies surveillance, increased mass dog vaccination, improved public awareness, and strengthened One Health collaboration. Such measures are essential for reducing human rabies deaths and supporting Ghana’s contribution to the global goal of eliminating dog-mediated human rabies by 2030.

Dog-mediated rabies remains a major neglected tropical disease and an important cause of preventable human mortality in sub-Saharan Africa, yet limited laboratory-confirmed surveillance data hinder accurate assessment of the disease burden and the development of evidence-based interventions in Ghana.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis was conducted using laboratory surveillance records of suspected rabies cases submitted to a regional veterinary laboratory in Ghana between 2020 and 2025, examining brain tissue samples from 51 suspected cases.
Core methods
Brain tissue samples were examined using Seller’s staining, the direct fluorescent antibody test (FAT), and polymerase chain reaction (PCR), while epidemiological information including animal species, vaccination history, ownership status, bite history, and diagnostic outcomes was extracted and analysed descriptively.
Main results
Of the 51 suspected cases examined, 46 (90.2%) were laboratory confirmed as rabies, with domestic dogs accounting for 96.1% of submissions and cats representing 3.9%, and confirmed cases were detected throughout the study period.
Interpretation and novelty
The consistently high proportion of laboratory-confirmed rabies cases demonstrates persistent transmission of rabies virus among domestic animals and highlights substantial risks to human health, while the integration of conventional and advanced laboratory diagnostic methods strengthened surveillance and improved case confirmation.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings underscore the urgent need to expand dog vaccination coverage, strengthen laboratory-based surveillance, improve bite reporting systems, and enhance collaboration between veterinary and public health sectors to accelerate progress towards the global goal of eliminating dog-mediated human rabies by 2030.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations, but the retrospective design and reliance on laboratory records may introduce selection bias and limit generalizability to other regions.

CAMKV 是一种 ISG,通过 SQSTM1 介导的选择性自噬促进狂犬病病毒磷蛋白的降解

CAMKV is an ISG and facilitates the degradation of rabies virus phosphoprotein via SQSTM1-mediated selective autophagy
作者： Jinliang Wang, Hong Huo, Yuting Tao, Xijun Wang, Zhiyuan Wen, Jinying Ge, Weiye Chen, Zhigao Bu, Lei Shuai
摘要中文翻译一旦狂犬病病毒(RABV)进入中枢神经系统,感染几乎不可避免地会导致致命的结果,而我们对病毒发病机制的不完全了解仍然是有效治疗干预的主要障碍。在这里,我们将 CAMKV 确定为干扰素刺激基因 (ISG),可驱动 RABV 磷蛋白 (P) 的巨自噬/自噬降解,从而在体外有效抑制病毒复制。值得注意的是,CAMKV 的体内过度表达显着延缓了接受 RABV 街头毒株攻击的小鼠的疾病进展。从机制上讲,CAMKV 与 RABV P 和 SQSTM1 相互作用,促进 SQSTM1 介导的 P 选择性自噬清除,从而限制 RABV 转录和复制。总的来说,我们的研究结果表明 CAMKV 是一种关键的宿主抗病毒效应子,通过选择性自噬发挥作用,强调 CAMKV 是开发针对致命性 RABV 感染的新型疗法的有前途的分子靶标

CAMKV is an ISG and facilitates the degradation of rabies virus phosphoprotein via SQSTM1-mediated selective autophagy

Authors: Jinliang Wang, Hong Huo, Yuting Tao, Xijun Wang, Zhiyuan Wen, Jinying Ge, Weiye Chen, Zhigao Bu, Lei Shuai
Original Abstract
Once rabies virus (RABV) gains access to the central nervous system, infection almost inevitably results in fatal outcomes, and our incomplete understanding of viral pathogenesis remains a major barrier to effective therapeutic intervention. Here, we identify CAMKV as an interferon-stimulated gene (ISG) that drives the macroautophagic/autophagic degradation of RABV phosphoprotein (P), thereby potently suppressing viral replication in vitro . Notably, in vivo overexpression of CAMKV significantly delays disease progression in mice challenged with a street strain of RABV. Mechanistically, CAMKV interacts with both RABV P and SQSTM1, promoting SQSTM1-mediated selective autophagic clearance of P and thereby restricting RABV transcription and replication. Collectively, our findings establish CAMKV as a critical host antiviral effector that functions through selective autophagy, highlighting CAMKV as a promising molecular target for the development of novel therapeutics against lethal RABV infection

Question and background
Once rabies virus (RABV) gains access to the central nervous system, infection almost inevitably results in fatal outcomes, and our incomplete understanding of viral pathogenesis remains a major barrier to effective therapeutic intervention.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.
Core methods
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the core experimental, analytical, or statistical methods.
Main results
Mechanistically, CAMKV interacts with both RABV P and SQSTM1, promoting SQSTM1-mediated selective autophagic clearance of P and thereby restricting RABV transcription and replication.
Interpretation and novelty
Collectively, our findings establish CAMKV as a critical host antiviral effector that functions through selective autophagy, highlighting CAMKV as a promising molecular target for the development of novel therapeutics against lethal RABV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
Notably, in vivo overexpression of CAMKV significantly delays disease progression in mice challenged with a street strain of RABV.
Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on retrieved fulltext evidence. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.

中国人用狂犬病疫苗上市后监测研究(2020-2024年)

A post-marketing surveillance study of a human rabies vaccine in China (2020–2024)
作者： Zhijie Cui, Lianfu Wang, Zhiyuan Ran, Yuan Liu, Yawei Gan, Wuyang Zhu
摘要中文翻译背景与目的 通过中国(2020-2024年)上市后监测评价益生君安®冻干人用狂犬病疫苗(Vero细胞)的安全性,重点关注不良反应模式和影响因素。方法 一项回顾性观察研究分析了来自国家直接不良事件报告系统 (DAERS) 的 2,419 例不良事件和制造商收集的数据。使用监管活动医学词典 (MedDRA) 进行标准化编码,按类型、严重程度和亚组(年龄、性别、剂量数等)对不良反应进行分类。采用 95% 置信区间对发病率进行分析,使用卡方检验评估关联性,并进行跨亚组比较,以阐明疫苗的安全性。结果 总共报告了 2,419 例 AE,总发生率为每 100,000 剂 7.406 例(95% CI 7.11–7.71)。全身反应占83.38%,局部反应占16.62%。发生17例严重不良事件(0.70%);没有死亡报告,全部康复。最常见的类别是一般疾病/给药部位病症(83.38%)和免疫系统疾病(5.70%)。AE 在第一次给药后最常见 (85.49%),并随着后续剂量的增加而显着下降。全身反应在儿童中更为常见(0-10岁:37.99%)。结论 益生君安®狂犬病疫苗具有良好的安全性,反应为轻至中度,支持其在暴露后预防中的应用。

A post-marketing surveillance study of a human rabies vaccine in China (2020–2024)

Authors: Zhijie Cui, Lianfu Wang, Zhiyuan Ran, Yuan Liu, Yawei Gan, Wuyang Zhu
Original Abstract
Background and objective To evaluate the safety profile of Yisheng Jun’an® freeze-dried human rabies vaccine (Vero cell) through post-marketing surveillance in China (2020–2024), focusing on adverse reaction patterns and influencing factors. Methods A retrospective observational study analyzed 2,419 adverse events from the national Direct Adverse Event Reporting System (DAERS) and manufacturer-collected data. Adverse reactions were categorized by type, severity, and subgroup (age, gender, dose number, etc.) using the Medical Dictionary for Regulatory Activities (MedDRA) for standardized coding. Analysis of incidence rates with 95% confidence intervals, use of the chi-square test to assess associations, and cross-subgroup comparisons were conducted to elucidate the safety profile of the vaccine. Results In total, 2,419 AEs were reported, yielding an overall incidence of 7.406 per 100,000 doses (95% CI 7.11–7.71). Systemic reactions comprised 83.38% and local reactions 16.62%. Seventeen serious adverse events (0.70%) occurred; no deaths were reported and all recovered. The most common categories were general disorders/administration-site conditions (83.38%) and immune system disorders (5.70%). AEs were most frequent after the first dose (85.49%) and declined markedly with subsequent doses. Systemic reactions were more common in children (0–10 years: 37.99%). Conclusion Yisheng Jun’an® rabies vaccine exhibits a favorable safety profile with mild-to-moderate reactions, supporting its use in post-exposure prophylaxis.

重组腺相关病毒血清型2介导的RNA干扰抑制狂犬病病毒复制

Recombinant adeno-associated virus serotype 2-mediated RNA interference inhibits rabies virus replication
作者： Yueming Wang, Yunsong Yu, Rongjie Huang, Songze Zhao, Yao Peng, Peirong Tan, Hongling He, Yue Zhang, Mengwei Zhang, Yongwen Luo
摘要中文翻译摘要 狂犬病病毒(RABV)是一种嗜神经病毒,可感染人类和动物的中枢神经系统,引起致命的神经症状并最终死亡。疫苗接种是预防狂犬病的唯一有效策略。然而,一旦出现临床症状,就没有有效的治疗方法。因此,探索有效的治疗方法,特别是暴露后预防势在必行。本研究调查了重组腺相关病毒血清型 2 (rAAV2) 递送的 siRNA 对 RABV 的抑制作用。设计了针对 RABV G 和 L 基因 mRNA 的特异性 siRNA。rAAV-G997 和 rAAV-L5055 是用 siRNA 表达盒构建的。在RABV感染之前用rAAV-G997或rAAV-L5055预处理神经母细胞瘤(NA)细胞导致病毒滴度以及G和L基因的mRNA水平显着降低。同样,在 RABV 感染之前用 rAAV-G997 或 rAAV-L5055 预处理幼仓鼠肾 (BHK-21) 细胞会降低病毒结构蛋白的表达。在小鼠模型中,与阴性对照(rAAV-NC)相比,颅内施用 rAAV-G997 或 rAAV-L5055 具有显着的保护作用。当在 RABV CVS-11 毒株攻击前施用时,rAAV-G997 和 rAAV-L5055 均显着降低了脑组织中的病毒载量,并实现了 90% 的存活率。同样,在暴露后治疗环境中,rAAV-G997 和 rAAV-L5055 组的存活率均为 88.9%。这些发现表明,rAAV 介导的针对 RABV G 和 L 基因的 siRNA 递送可以在体外抑制病毒复制,并在体内提供显着的保护,凸显了 rAAV2 作为治疗狂犬病的治疗平台的潜力。

Recombinant adeno-associated virus serotype 2-mediated RNA interference inhibits rabies virus replication

Authors: Yueming Wang, Yunsong Yu, Rongjie Huang, Songze Zhao, Yao Peng, Peirong Tan, Hongling He, Yue Zhang, Mengwei Zhang, Yongwen Luo
Original Abstract
Abstract Rabies virus (RABV) is a neurotropic virus that infects the central nervous systems of humans and animals, causing fatal neurological symptoms and ultimately death. Vaccination is the only effective strategy for preventing rabies. However, once clinical symptoms appear, there is no effective treatment. Therefore, exploring effective therapeutic approaches, particularly for postexposure prophylaxis, is imperative. This study investigated the inhibitory effect of siRNA delivered by recombinant adeno-associated virus serotype 2 (rAAV2) against RABV. Specific siRNAs targeting the mRNA of the G and L genes of the RABV were designed. rAAV-G997 and rAAV-L5055 were constructed with siRNA expression cassettes. Pretreatment of neuroblastoma (NA) cells with either rAAV-G997 or rAAV-L5055 before RABV infection resulted in a considerable reduction in both the viral titer and the mRNA levels of the G and L genes. Similarly, pretreatment of baby hamster kidney (BHK-21) cells with rAAV-G997 or rAAV-L5055 prior to RABV infection decreased the expression of viral structural proteins. In a mouse model, compared with the negative control (rAAV-NC), the intracranial administration of rAAV-G997 or rAAV-L5055 conferred significant protection. When administered before challenge with the RABV CVS-11 strain, both rAAV-G997 and rAAV-L5055 significantly reduced the viral load in brain tissue, and achieved a survival rate of 90%. Similarly, in a postexposure treatment setting, both the rAAV-G997 and rAAV-L5055 groups achieved a survival rate of 88.9%. These findings demonstrate that rAAV-mediated delivery of siRNAs targeting the RABV G and L genes can inhibit viral replication in vitro and provide significant protection in vivo, highlighting the potential of rAAV2 as a therapeutic platform for treating rabies.

Main results

Interpretation and novelty

印度尼西亚东努沙登加拉省沃洛梅泽分区 Natarandang 社区卫生中心为被狗咬伤的患者注射狂犬病疫苗的情况

Profile of Rabies Vaccination Administration for Patients Bitten by Dogs at the Natarandang Community Health Centre, Wolomeze Sub-district, East Nusa Tenggara, Indonesia
作者： Sri Natalia Ginting
摘要中文翻译简介:狂犬病是一种病毒性疾病,一旦出现临床症状,几乎总是致命的,而狗是主要传播源。本研究旨在描述沃洛梅兹区 Natarandang 健康中心被狗咬伤的患者的狂犬病疫苗接种情况。方法:对 2022 年 1 月至 2023 年 2 月期间 32 名被狗咬伤后接受狂犬病疫苗接种的患者进行回顾性描述性研究。从病历中收集数据并进行描述性分析。结果:大多数患者为男性(65.6%),最常见的咬伤部位为小腿(34.4%)和手(28.1%)。最常见的急救措施包括检查狗的感染史和就医(各占34.4%)。最常用的狂犬病疫苗是 PCECV(81.3%)。延误医疗护理与发烧(65.6%)为主要症状有关。结论:Natarandang 健康中心的狗咬伤患者大多数为男性,最常见的咬伤部位是小腿和手部。最常报告的急救方法是检查狗的病史并寻求医疗救助。主要接种的狂犬病疫苗是 PCECV。发烧是最常见的症状。

Profile of Rabies Vaccination Administration for Patients Bitten by Dogs at the Natarandang Community Health Centre, Wolomeze Sub-district, East Nusa Tenggara, Indonesia

Authors: Sri Natalia Ginting
Original Abstract
Introduction: Rabies is a viral disease that is almost always fatal once clinical symptoms appear, with dogs being the primary source of transmission. This study aims to describe the rabies vaccination profile of dog bite patients at Natarandang Health Center, Wolomeze District. Methods: A retrospective descriptive study on 32 patients who received rabies vaccination after dog bites between January 2022 and February 2023. Data were collected from medical records and analyzed descriptively. Results: Most patients were male (65.6%), with the most common bite locations in the calf (34.4%) and hand (28.1%). The most frequent first aid measures included checking the dog's infection history and seeking medical attention (34.4% each). The most commonly administered rabies vaccine was PCECV (81.3%). Delayed medical care was associated with fever (65.6%) as the predominant symptom. Conclusion: Most dog bite patients at Natarandang Health Center were male, with the most common bite locations in the calf and hand. The most frequently reported first aid were checking the dog’s history and seeking medical attention. The predominant rabies vaccine administered was PCECV. Fever was the most common symptom.

RVG功能化小胶质细胞膜包被的铈没食子酸金属有机框架用于多靶点阿尔茨海默病治疗

RVG-functionalized microglial membrane–coated cerium–gallic acid metal–organic frameworks for multi-target Alzheimer's therapy
作者： Fengmei Yang, Yuehua Chen, Yujiao Yan, Ruixin Zhao, Liran Deng, Die Tian, Qi Wang, Meng Xie
摘要中文翻译阿尔茨海默病(AD)是一种多因素神经退行性疾病,其特征是异常的β-淀粉样蛋白(Aβ)聚集、氧化应激、神经炎症和金属离子稳态破坏,这些限制了传统单靶点疗法的疗效。为了解决这些相互交织的病理过程,我们开发了一种基于铈没食子酸生物金属有机框架(CeGA-MOF)的多功能纳米治疗平台。可逆的 Ce 3+ /Ce 4+ 氧化还原循环能够有效清除活性氧 (ROS),而没食子酸既可以作为有机配体,也可以作为金属螯合剂,抑制金属离子介导的 Aβ 聚集,减轻氧化应激相关的神经毒性,并具有内在的抗炎活性。为了增强体内稳定性和脑输送,CeGA-MOF 涂有小胶质细胞膜,并用狂犬病病毒糖蛋白 (RVG) 肽进行功能化,产生仿生纳米系统 CeGA-MOF/B/R。小胶质细胞膜提供免疫逃避和炎症引导的靶向,而 RVG 则促进血脑屏障穿透。在体外,CeGA-MOF/B/R有效螯合Cu 2+ 、Zn 2+ 和Fe 3+,抑制金属离子诱导的Aβ聚集,并保护神经元。在 APP/PS1 转基因小鼠中,它减少了大脑 Aβ,促进抗炎小胶质细胞极化,并改善学习和记忆,凸显了其作为协同 AD 治疗的仿生纳米平台的潜力。

RVG-functionalized microglial membrane–coated cerium–gallic acid metal–organic frameworks for multi-target Alzheimer's therapy

Authors: Fengmei Yang, Yuehua Chen, Yujiao Yan, Ruixin Zhao, Liran Deng, Die Tian, Qi Wang, Meng Xie
Original Abstract
Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a multifactorial neurodegenerative disorder characterized by aberrant β-amyloid (Aβ) aggregation, oxidative stress, neuroinflammation, and disrupted metal ion homeostasis, which limit the efficacy of conventional single-target therapies. To address these intertwined pathological processes, we developed a multifunctional nanotherapeutic platform based on a cerium-gallic acid bio-metal-organic framework (CeGA-MOF). Reversible Ce 3+ /Ce 4+ redox cycling enables efficient reactive oxygen species (ROS) scavenging, while gallic acid serves as both an organic ligand and metal chelator, inhibiting metal ion-mediated Aβ aggregation and alleviating oxidative stress-associated neurotoxicity, along with its intrinsic anti-inflammatory activity. To enhance in vivo stability and brain delivery, CeGA-MOF was coated with microglial membranes and functionalized with rabies virus glycoprotein (RVG) peptide, yielding a biomimetic nanosystem, CeGA-MOF/B/R. The microglial membrane provides immune evasion and inflammation-guided targeting, while RVG facilitates blood-brain barrier penetration. In vitro, CeGA-MOF/B/R effectively chelates Cu 2+ , Zn 2+ , and Fe 3+ , suppresses metal ion-induced Aβ aggregation, and protects neurons. In APP/PS1 transgenic mice, it reduced cerebral Aβ, promoted anti-inflammatory microglial polarization, and improved learning and memory, highlighting its potential as a biomimetic nanoplatform for synergistic AD therapy.

Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a multifactorial neurodegenerative disorder characterized by aberrant β-amyloid (Aβ) aggregation, oxidative stress, neuroinflammation, and disrupted metal ion homeostasis, which limit the efficacy of conventional single-target therapies.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.
Core methods
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the core experimental, analytical, or statistical methods.
Main results
Reversible Ce 3+ /Ce 4+ redox cycling enables efficient reactive oxygen species (ROS) scavenging, while gallic acid serves as both an organic ligand and metal chelator, inhibiting metal ion-mediated Aβ aggregation and alleviating oxidative stress-associated neurotoxicity, along with its intrinsic anti-inflammatory activity.
Interpretation and novelty
In vitro, CeGA-MOF/B/R effectively chelates Cu 2+ , Zn 2+ , and Fe 3+ , suppresses metal ion-induced Aβ aggregation, and protects neurons.
Scientific and public-health significance
In APP/PS1 transgenic mice, it reduced cerebral Aβ, promoted anti-inflammatory microglial polarization, and improved learning and memory, highlighting its potential as a biomimetic nanoplatform for synergistic AD therapy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The microglial membrane provides immune evasion and inflammation-guided targeting, while RVG facilitates blood-brain barrier penetration.

Alphavirus madariaga-associated encephalomyelitis in equines from the Brazilian Pantanal reveals arbovirus circulation in a peri-urban environment
作者： Luana Dias do Nascimento, Tarcísio Ávila Dos Santos, Tiago Baldin, Rachel Vieira Paes de Barros, Sayanne Luns Hatum de Almeida, Nathalia Assis Pereira, Luciano Nakazato, Jean-Paul Carrera, Carolina Argenta Pescador, Edson Moleta Colodel
摘要中文翻译马达里亚加甲病毒 (MADV) 是东部马脑炎病毒复合体的成员,是南美洲一种新兴的虫媒病毒,有关其在马群体中的发生和分子特征的信息有限。本研究报告了巴西马托格罗索州马科动物 MADV 的检测和遗传特征。对两匹患有急性神经系统疾病、临床病程大约为两天的马进行了评估。组织病理学检查显示非化脓性脑膜脑脊髓炎。使用 TaqMan RT-qPCR 检测对嗜神经病毒进行分子筛查,狂犬病病毒、EHV-1、WEEV、VEEV、WNV 和黄病毒呈阴性,而检测到 MADV,Ct 值为 27.9 和 31.4。部分 NS1 和 E1 基因片段被扩增并测序。基于这两个基因的系统发育分析一致地将名为 Santo Antonio 的毒株置于 MADV 基因型 III 进化枝内,并在 E1 分析中得到了强有力的支持。这些病例发生在圣安东尼奥杜莱韦尔热附近的城郊地区,距离库亚巴约 30 公里,凸显了森林环境与人类改造环境之间的潜在界面。这些发现提供了巴西中西部 MADV 循环及其与马科疾病关联的分子证据。我们的研究结果强化了马作为嗜神经性虫媒病毒监测哨兵在到达人类之前的作用。

Alphavirus madariaga-associated encephalomyelitis in equines from the Brazilian Pantanal reveals arbovirus circulation in a peri-urban environment

Authors: Luana Dias do Nascimento, Tarcísio Ávila Dos Santos, Tiago Baldin, Rachel Vieira Paes de Barros, Sayanne Luns Hatum de Almeida, Nathalia Assis Pereira, Luciano Nakazato, Jean-Paul Carrera, Carolina Argenta Pescador, Edson Moleta Colodel
Original Abstract
Alphavirus madariaga (MADV), a member of the eastern equine encephalitis virus complex, is an emerging arbovirus in South America, with limited information regarding its occurrence and molecular characterization in equine populations. This study reports the detection and genetic characterization of MADV in equines from the state of Mato Grosso, Brazil. Two horses presenting acute neurological disease with a clinical course of approximately two days were evaluated. Histopathological examination revealed nonsuppurative meningoencephalomyelitis. Molecular screening for neurotropic viruses using TaqMan RT-qPCR assays was negative for rabies virus, EHV-1, WEEV, VEEV, WNV, and flaviviruses, while MADV was detected with Ct values of 27.9 and 31.4. Partial NS1 and E1 gene fragments were amplified and sequenced. Phylogenetic analyses based on both genes consistently placed the strain, named Santo Antonio, within the MADV genotype III clade, with strong support in the E1 analysis. The cases occurred in a peri-urban area located near Santo Antônio do Leverger, approximately 30 km from Cuiabá, highlighting a potential interface between sylvatic and human-modified environments. These findings provide molecular evidence of MADV circulation in central-western Brazil and association with equines disease. Our results reinforce the role of horses as sentinels for neurotropic arbovirus surveillance before reaching human population.

Interpretation and novelty

单神经元分辨率的集成输入输出连接组学揭示了小鼠腹侧后外侧核中的平行投影流。

Integrated Input-Output Connectomics at Single-Neuron Resolution Reveals Parallel Projection Streams in the Mouse Ventral Posterolateral Nucleus.
作者： Hengzheng Wei, Yunqing Li, Tian-Yu Zhao, Xiao Ren, Xin-Tong Qiu, Fu-xing Zhang, Mingming Zhang
摘要中文翻译腹侧后外侧核(VPL)长期以来被认为是从脊髓到初级体感皮层(SSp)的体感信息的线性中继。然而,非感觉信号(包括运动命令和情感状态)的 VPL 集成基础电路架构仍然没有明确定义;特别是,该核内由囊泡谷氨酸转运蛋白 2 (VGluT2) 标记的典型兴奋性神经元的相关回路机制在很大程度上仍不清楚。通过将单突触狂犬病追踪与荧光微光学断层扫描 (fMOST) 相结合,我们生成了小鼠 VPL VGluT2 神经元的定量、单细胞分辨率连接组。我们发现这些神经元接收来自运动、边缘和基底神经节回路的大量汇聚输入,挑战了 VPL 是单峰感觉中继的经典观点。单神经元重建 (n=149) 进一步揭示了四种不同的投射亚型,它们不仅支配 SSp,还支配初级运动皮层 (MOp) 和高阶关联区域。值得注意的是,对 MOp 的投影优先针对第 5 层,绕过规范的感觉层次结构。这些解剖学发现与 VPL 可能作为感觉运动整合枢纽的假设是一致的,并且它们为理解体感如何与行动和认知相互作用提供了关键的结构基础。

Integrated Input-Output Connectomics at Single-Neuron Resolution Reveals Parallel Projection Streams in the Mouse Ventral Posterolateral Nucleus.

Authors: Hengzheng Wei, Yunqing Li, Tian-Yu Zhao, Xiao Ren, Xin-Tong Qiu, Fu-xing Zhang, Mingming Zhang
Original Abstract
The ventral posterolateral nucleus (VPL) has long been regarded as a linear relay of somatosensory information from the spinal cord to the primary somatosensory cortex (SSp). However, the circuit architecture underlying VPL integration of non-sensory signals, including motor commands and affective states, remains poorly defined; in particular, the relevant circuit mechanisms of canonical excitatory neurons labeled by vesicular glutamate transporter 2 (VGluT2) within this nucleus remain largely unelucidated. By combining monosynaptic rabies tracing with fluorescence micro-optical sectioning tomography (fMOST), we generated a quantitative, single-cell resolution connectome of VPL VGluT2 neurons in mice. We find that these neurons receive substantial convergent inputs from motor, limbic, and basal ganglia circuits, challenging the classical view that VPL is a unimodal sensory relay. Single-neuron reconstructions (n=149) further reveal four distinct projection subtypes that innervate not only the SSp but also the primary motor cortex (MOp) and higher-order association areas. Notably, projections to MOp preferentially target layer 5, bypassing the canonical sensory hierarchy. These anatomical findings are consistent with the hypothesis that the VPL may serve as a hub for sensorimotor integration, and they provide a critical structural basis for understanding how somatosensation interfaces with action and cognition.

超越暴发计数:使用病例损失率和每次暴发病例数比率对主要动物疾病进行新的流行病学分类

Beyond Outbreak Counts: A Novel Epidemiological Classification of Major Animal Diseases Using Loss-to-Case and Cases-per-Outbreak Ratios
作者： Arun HS Kumar
摘要中文翻译2026年世界动物卫生组织(WOAH)《世界动物健康状况》报告第2版提供了关于影响牲畜、家禽、野生动物和伴侣动物的传染病全球负担的关键见解。虽然传统流行病学指标,如疫情数量、病例数和死亡数,对于疾病监测仍然至关重要,但它们无法完全捕捉与不同疾病相关的严重程度和传播动态。本报告引入了两个额外的流行病学指标:损失/病例比和病例/疫情比。损失/病例比通过将总动物损失或扑杀事件与确诊病例进行比较,评估疾病的相对严重程度和死亡负担,而病例/疫情比通过测量每次疫情的平均病例数,估算疫情强度和传播规模。利用这些参数,将十大主要动物疾病分为高、中、低影响类别。由于极高的死亡率和扑杀负担,绵羊痘和山羊痘以及家禽禽流感被确定为高损失疾病。家禽禽流感被确定为唯一的高疫情疾病,反映了集约化家禽生产系统相关的极端传播强度。非洲猪瘟、口蹄疫和小反刍兽疫表现出中等疫情强度,而狂犬病和新世界螺旋锥蝇等疾病尽管地理分布广泛,但疫情集中度相对较低。研究结果表明,将这些新指标纳入动物健康监测系统可以改善疾病优先级排序,加强风险评估,并支持更有针对性的预防和控制策略。这一分类框架提供了对疾病行为和全球动物健康威胁的更全面理解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2026年世界动物卫生组织(WOAH)《世界动物健康状况》报告第2版提供了关于影响牲畜、家禽、野生动物和伴侣动物的传染病全球负担的关键见解。
设计与对象
本报告引入了另外两个流行病学指标:损失/病例比率和病例/暴发比率。
核心方法
利用这些参数,将十种主要动物疾病分为高、中、低影响类别。
主要结果
非洲猪瘟、口蹄疫、小反刍兽疫等疫情爆发强度适中,而狂犬病、新世界螺旋虫等疾病尽管地理分布广泛,但疫情集中度相对较低。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,将这些新指标纳入动物健康监测系统可以改善疾病优先顺序、加强风险评估并支持更有针对性的预防和控制策略。
科研与公卫意义
虽然疫情、病例和死亡数量等传统流行病学指标对于疾病监测仍然至关重要,但它们并不能完全反映与不同疾病相关的严重程度和传播动态。
局限与证据强度
该分类框架提供了对疾病行为和全球动物健康威胁的更全面的了解。

Beyond Outbreak Counts: A Novel Epidemiological Classification of Major Animal Diseases Using Loss-to-Case and Cases-per-Outbreak Ratios

Authors: Arun HS Kumar
Original Abstract
The 2026 World Organisation for Animal Health (WOAH) 2nd edition of State of the World’s Animal Health report provides critical insight into the global burden of infectious diseases affecting livestock, poultry, wildlife, and companion animals. While traditional epidemiological indicators such as the number of outbreaks, cases, and deaths remain essential for disease monitoring, they do not fully capture the severity and transmission dynamics associated with different diseases. This report introduces two additional epidemiological indicators: the loss/cases ratio and the cases/outbreaks ratio. The loss/cases ratio evaluates the relative severity and mortality burden of a disease by comparing total animal losses or culling events to confirmed cases, whereas the cases/outbreaks ratio estimates outbreak intensity and transmission scale by measuring the average number of cases per outbreak. Using these parameters, the ten major animal diseases were classified into high-, medium-, and low-impact categories. Sheep pox and goat pox together with Avian influenza in poultry emerged as high-loss diseases due to their exceptionally high mortality and culling burden. Avian influenza in poultry was identified as the only high-outbreak disease, reflecting the extreme transmission intensity associated with intensive poultry production systems. African swine fever, Foot and mouth disease, and Peste des petits ruminants demonstrated moderate outbreak intensity, while diseases such as Rabies and New World screwworm showed comparatively lower outbreak concentration despite wide geographic distribution. The findings demonstrate that integrating these new indicators into animal health surveillance systems can improve disease prioritisation, strengthen risk assessment, and support more targeted prevention and control strategies. This classification framework provides a more comprehensive understanding of disease behaviour and global animal health threats.
View research seven-element analysis

Limitations and evidence strength

开发一种新型猫科动物组合疫苗,包括针对猫科动物白血病病毒的新型 RNA 颗粒疫苗

Development of a New Feline Combination Vaccine Including a Novel RNA Particle Vaccine Against Feline Leukemia Virus
作者： Willem Huisman, Aart Mommen, Stephanie Basten, Kim Driessen, Peter Pepels, Qi Cao, Hans Holtslag, Melissa Bourgeois, Jacqueline Pearce
摘要中文翻译背景/目标:我们描述了一种新型猫科动物联合疫苗 (Nobivac® NXT HCPChFeLV) 的基本原理、设计和评估,该疫苗结合了针对猫科白血病病毒 (FeLV) 的 RNA 颗粒技术以及针对猫科动物疱疹病毒 1 (FHV)、猫科杯状病毒 (FCV)、猫科泛白细胞减少症 (FPL) 和猫衣原体的活减毒成分。介绍了实验室和现场研究的功效和安全性结果。方法:Nobivac NXT HCPChFeLV 是一种 0.5 mL 无佐剂冻干疫苗,含有减毒活 FHV、FCV、FPL 和猫 C. felis 菌株,以及表达 FeLV 包膜糖蛋白 (gp85) 的甲病毒复制子衍生的 RNA 颗粒。主要疫苗接种计划包括在 8-9 周龄时进行皮下疫苗接种,并在 4 周后进行加强疫苗接种。初次接种疫苗后 1 周进行免疫起始研究。免疫持续时间研究评估了初次接种疫苗后 1 年和 3 年的攻击情况,并在 1 年后加强接种(FHV、FCV、FeLV);FPL 持续时间在初次疫苗接种后 3 年进行评估;C. felis 持续时间评估为 1 年。评估疾病、生存、白细胞减少症(FPL)和病原体排泄的临床症状。在实验室过量/重复剂量研究和现场研究中评估了安全性,该研究比较了狂犬病疫苗接种与阳性对照方案的同时(非混合)使用。结果:疫苗接种显着减少了 FHV、FCV 和 C. felis 攻击后的临床疾病,并降低了 FHV 相关死亡率。接种疫苗的猫可以免受 FPL 临床疾病、死亡率、白细胞减少症和 FPL 排泄的影响。在初次接种疫苗后和为期 1 年的免疫研究中,持续性 FeLV 抗原血症得到显着预防,并且在重新接种疫苗 3 年后减少。与对照组相比,接种疫苗的人 FHV、FCV 和 C. felis 的排泄量显着减少。实验室研究中未观察到严重不良事件;现场疫苗接种耐受性良好,没有报告注射部位反应。结论:Nobivac NXT HCPChFeLV 可针对多种猫科病原体提供强大、持久的保护,减少病原体脱落,并表现出良好的安全性,支持其在猫科疾病预防保健中的使用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景/目标:我们描述了一种新型猫科动物联合疫苗 (Nobivac® NXT HCPChFeLV) 的基本原理、设计和评估,该疫苗结合了针对猫科白血病病毒 (FeLV) 的 RNA 颗粒技术以及针对猫科动物疱疹病毒 1 (FHV)、猫科杯状病毒 (FCV)、猫科泛白细胞减少症 (FPL) 和猫衣原体的活减毒成分。
设计与对象
在实验室过量/重复剂量研究和现场研究中评估了安全性,该研究比较了狂犬病疫苗接种与阳性对照方案的同时(非混合)使用。
核心方法
免疫持续时间研究评估了初次接种疫苗后 1 年和 3 年的攻击情况,并在 1 年后加强接种(FHV、FCV、FeLV);FPL 持续时间在初次疫苗接种后 3 年进行评估;C.
主要结果
猫科动物挑战并降低了 FHV 相关死亡率。
解释与创新
FHV、FCV和C的排泄。
科研与公卫意义
Nobivac NXT HCPChFeLV 可针对多种猫科病原体提供强大、持久的保护,减少病原体脱落,并表现出良好的安全性,支持其在猫科疾病预防保健中的使用。

Development of a New Feline Combination Vaccine Including a Novel RNA Particle Vaccine Against Feline Leukemia Virus

Authors: Willem Huisman, Aart Mommen, Stephanie Basten, Kim Driessen, Peter Pepels, Qi Cao, Hans Holtslag, Melissa Bourgeois, Jacqueline Pearce
Original Abstract
Background/Objectives: We describe the rationale, design and evaluation of a novel feline combination vaccine (Nobivac® NXT HCPChFeLV) incorporating RNA particle technology against feline leukemia virus (FeLV) alongside live attenuated components for feline herpesvirus-1 (FHV), feline calicivirus (FCV), feline panleukopenia (FPL) and Chlamydia felis. Efficacy and safety outcomes from laboratory and field studies are presented. Methods: Nobivac NXT HCPChFeLV is a 0.5 mL, non-adjuvanted, lyophilized vaccine containing live attenuated FHV, FCV, FPL, and C. felis strains, and an alphavirus replicon-derived RNA particle expressing FeLV envelope glycoprotein (gp85). The primary vaccination schedule involved subcutaneous vaccination at 8–9 weeks of age with a booster 4 weeks later. Onset-of-immunity studies were performed 1 week post primary vaccination. Duration-of-immunity studies evaluated challenge both at 1 year and 3 years after primary vaccination with a booster at 1 year (FHV, FCV, FeLV); FPL duration was assessed at 3 years after primary vaccination; C. felis duration was assessed at 1 year. Clinical signs of disease, survival, leukopenia (FPL) and pathogen excretion were assessed. Safety was assessed in laboratory overdose/repeat-dose studies and in a field study comparing concurrent (non-mixed) use with rabies vaccination vs a positive control regimen. Results: Vaccination significantly reduced clinical disease following FHV, FCV and C. felis challenge and reduced FHV-associated mortality. Vaccinated cats were protected from FPL clinical disease, mortality, leukopenia, and FPL excretion. Persistent FeLV antigenemia was significantly prevented after primary vaccination and in 1-year-duration-of-immunity studies and was reduced 3 years after revaccination. The excretion of FHV, FCV and C. felis was significantly reduced in vaccinates vs controls. No serious adverse events were observed in laboratory studies; field vaccination was well tolerated with no injection-site reactions reported. Conclusions: Nobivac NXT HCPChFeLV provides robust, long-lasting protection against multiple feline pathogens, reduced pathogen shedding and demonstrated a favorable safety profile, supporting its use in feline preventive healthcare.

在巴斯德博物馆保存路易斯巴斯德的实验室玻璃器皿

Preserving Louis Pasteur’s Laboratory Glassware at the Pasteur Museum
作者： Viviane Miceski, Esther Jorel, Laurence Isnard
摘要中文翻译巴斯德博物馆的科学纪念品室于 1938 年落成,收藏了近 300 件路易斯巴斯德科学工作中使用的玻璃器皿和实验室仪器,从他对分子不对称的研究到狂犬病疫苗的开发。作为他实验实践的直接见证,这些物品结合了吹制玻璃、软木、蜡、树脂、橡胶和手写标签。2024 年至 2025 年间进行的保护项目提供了一个独特的机会来记录这些藏品的物质状况、调查现有的改变并确定损坏的主要因素。这项研究重点关注含有液体的玻璃器皿,强调了玻璃的易碎性,并强调了环境参数对其保存的重要性。它是实施预防性保护战略和这一特殊科学遗产长期监测计划的基础。

Preserving Louis Pasteur’s Laboratory Glassware at the Pasteur Museum

Authors: Viviane Miceski, Esther Jorel, Laurence Isnard
Original Abstract
The Scientific Souvenirs Room in the Pasteur Museum, inaugurated in 1938, houses nearly 300 examples of glassware and laboratory instruments used in Louis Pasteur’s scientific work, from his studies on molecular asymmetry to his development of the rabies vaccine. As direct witnesses to his experimental practice, these objects combine blown glass, cork, wax, resin, rubber, and handwritten labels. The conservation project carried out between 2024 and 2025 provided a unique opportunity to document the material condition of this collection, investigate the existing alterations, and identify the main factors of deterioration. This study, focused on glassware containing liquids, highlights the fragility of glass and underscores the importance of environmental parameters in its preservation. It serves as a foundation for the implementation of a preventive conservation strategy and a long-term monitoring plan for this exceptional scientific heritage.

非编码RNA在弹状病毒科病毒引起的感染中的调节作用

Regulatory role of noncoding RNAs in infections caused by Rhabdoviridae family viruses
作者： Fatemeh Zahra Mohammadzadeh, Anahita Nabavi, Mohammad Karimian
摘要中文翻译弹状病毒科由负义单链 RNA 病毒组成,可在多种宿主中引起严重感染。越来越多的证据表明,宿主非编码 RNA (ncRNA),包括 microRNA (miRNA)、长链非编码 RNA (lncRNA) 和环状 RNA (circRNA),在调节弹状病毒复制、发病机制和宿主免疫反应中发挥着核心作用。这些 ncRNA 通过调节先天免疫信号通路、基因表达的调节和炎症反应的控制来发挥抗病毒和前病毒作用。抗病毒 ncRNA 通过促进干扰素产生和干扰素刺激基因以及激活炎症和抗病毒防御途径来抑制病毒复制。另一方面,前病毒 ncRNA 通过调节或抑制 NF-κB 和 JAK/STAT 等关键信号通路来促进病毒存活和复制。这篇综述提供了弹状病毒感染中 ncRNA 介导的调控机制的以通路为中心的综合,特别强调了金迪普拉病毒、水泡性口炎病毒和狂犬病病毒。通过整合目前关于 ncRNA 和弹状病毒之间细胞和分子相互作用的研究结果,这篇综述强调了 ncRNA 在病毒发病机制和宿主免疫中的双重调节作用,并讨论了它们作为控制弹状病毒感染的治疗靶点和生物标志物的潜力。

这篇综述提供了弹状病毒感染中 ncRNA 介导的调控机制的以通路为中心的综合,特别强调了金迪普拉病毒、水泡性口炎病毒和狂犬病病毒。
证据基础与方法
提供的证据没有明确说明审查方法、数据库、资格流程或证据基础。
共识与结论
越来越多的证据表明,宿主非编码 RNA (ncRNA),包括 microRNA (miRNA)、长链非编码 RNA (lncRNA) 和环状 RNA (circRNA),在调节弹状病毒复制、发病机制和宿主免疫反应中发挥着核心作用。
争议与缺口
另一方面,前病毒 ncRNA 通过调节或抑制 NF-κB 和 JAK/STAT 等关键信号通路来促进病毒存活和复制。
科研与实践启示
通过整合目前关于 ncRNA 和弹状病毒之间细胞和分子相互作用的研究结果,这篇综述强调了 ncRNA 在病毒发病机制和宿主免疫中的双重调节作用,并讨论了它们作为控制弹状病毒感染的治疗靶点和生物标志物的潜力。

Regulatory role of noncoding RNAs in infections caused by Rhabdoviridae family viruses

Authors: Fatemeh Zahra Mohammadzadeh, Anahita Nabavi, Mohammad Karimian
Original Abstract
The Rhabdoviridae family comprises negative-sense single-stranded RNA viruses that cause severe infections in a wide range of hosts. Increasing evidence indicates that host noncoding RNAs (ncRNAs), including microRNAs (miRNAs), long noncoding RNAs (lncRNAs), and circular RNAs (circRNAs), play central roles in regulating rhabdovirus replication, pathogenesis, and host immune responses. These ncRNAs exert both antiviral and proviral effects through modulation of innate immune signaling pathways, regulation of gene expression, and control of inflammatory responses. Antiviral ncRNAs suppress viral replication by promoting interferon production and interferon-stimulated genes and by activating inflammatory and antiviral defense pathways. On the other hand, proviral ncRNAs facilitate viral survival and replication through modulation or inhibition of key signaling pathways such as NF-κB and JAK/STAT. This review provides a pathway-centric synthesis of ncRNA-mediated regulatory mechanisms in rhabdovirus infections, with particular emphasis on Chandipura virus, vesicular stomatitis virus, and rabies virus. By integrating current findings on the cellular and molecular interactions between ncRNAs and rhabdoviruses, this review highlights the dual regulatory roles of ncRNAs in viral pathogenesis and host immunity and discusses their potential as therapeutic targets and biomarkers for the control of rhabdoviral infections.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review provides a pathway-centric synthesis of ncRNA-mediated regulatory mechanisms in rhabdovirus infections, with particular emphasis on Chandipura virus, vesicular stomatitis virus, and rabies virus.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the review method, databases, eligibility process, or evidence base.
Consensus and conclusions
Increasing evidence indicates that host noncoding RNAs (ncRNAs), including microRNAs (miRNAs), long noncoding RNAs (lncRNAs), and circular RNAs (circRNAs), play central roles in regulating rhabdovirus replication, pathogenesis, and host immune responses.
Controversies and gaps
On the other hand, proviral ncRNAs facilitate viral survival and replication through modulation or inhibition of key signaling pathways such as NF-κB and JAK/STAT.
Research and practice implications
By integrating current findings on the cellular and molecular interactions between ncRNAs and rhabdoviruses, this review highlights the dual regulatory roles of ncRNAs in viral pathogenesis and host immunity and discusses their potential as therapeutic targets and biomarkers for the control of rhabdoviral infections.

对 2005-2025 年“同一个健康”干预措施的范围审查:已经做了哪些工作,这些措施是否有效,以及最佳实践是什么?

A scoping review of One Health interventions 2005–2025: What has been done, are they effective, and what are the best practices?
作者： Eri Togami, Lincoln Hopkins, Lori Rosman, Meghan F. Davis
摘要中文翻译背景:“同一个健康”研究和实践正在迅速发展,需要对“同一个健康”干预措施建立可靠的证据和质量评估。我们进行了范围界定审查,目的是 (1) 描述同行评审文献中发表的 One Health 干预措施的数量和类型;(2) 评估所确定的干预措施是否有效。我们假设证据的数量和证明有效性的能力有限。方法:根据系统评价的首选报告项目和范围界定评价的荟萃分析扩展来报告评价。最初的检索于 2024 年 5 月在 Medline (Ovid)、Embase、PubAg 和 Web of Science 上进行,并于 2025 年 12 月更新。纳入标准为: 研究人员干预人类、动物或其共享环境(三个领域中的至少两个)的主要研究;其中包括结果评估,全文有英文版,于 2025 年 12 月 16 日或之前发表。两名研究人员独立筛选和分析了所有手稿。结果:筛选了超过 18,000 篇参考文献,纳入了 151 项研究。研究在代表世卫组织所有六个区域的 53 个国家进行。据报告,美国、坦桑尼亚和印度的干预次数最多。在 79 项针对人畜共患疾病的干预措施中,最常见的疾病是一般人畜共患病、狂犬病和囊虫病。超过一半 (55%) 的研究设计是准实验性的,其次是 23% 的随机对照试验 (RCT) 和 19% 的非随机实验。不到一半 (41%) 的研究设有对照组。最常见的偏见来源是移民(28%)和移民选择偏见(11%)。八项基于现场的随机对照试验表明,与对照相比,“同一个健康”干预措施可有效减少疾病发生或病原体检测。典型的实践包括跨越人类、动物和环境领域的综合干预,使用参与式方法,评估长期结果,使用多种方法对结果进行三角测量,不夸大发现,并提供视觉辅助来描述干预措施。结论:少数实地随机对照试验清楚地证明了“同一个健康”干预措施的相对有效性,而在没有更细粒度的有效性定义的情况下,评估其他研究的有效性具有挑战性。评估“同一个健康”干预措施有效性的严格研究将共同​​提高政府、研究人员和政策制定者的支持度,并推进采用“同一个健康”方法的证据基础。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该审查审查了针对人畜共患疾病的“同一个健康”干预措施,重点关注其在人类、动物和环境领域的有效性和最佳实践。核心问题涉及已发表的干预措施的特征及其在同行评审文献中所证明的有效性。
证据基础与方法
本次范围界定审查遵循 PRISMA-ScR 指南,于 2024 年 5 月检索了 Medline (Ovid)、Embase、PubAg 和 Web of Science,并于 2025 年 12 月更新。纳入标准要求进行初步研究,干预措施至少涵盖三个单一健康领域(人类、动物、环境)中的两个、结果评估和英文全文,于 2025 年 12 月 16 日出版。两名研究人员独立筛选和分析手稿。
共识与结论
八项实地随机对照试验 (RCT) 表明,与对照相比,One Health 干预措施有效减少了疾病发生或病原体检测。该审查强调,一小部分但严格的研究提供了有效性的明确证据,而更广泛的评估因各研究对有效性的定义不一致而受到阻碍。
争议与缺口
不到一半 (41%) 的研究包括对照组,超过一半 (55%) 使用准实验设计,限制了因果推断。选择偏见很常见,移民(28%)和移民(11%)偏见经常被察觉。缺乏对有效性的精细定义使交叉研究比较变得复杂,并削弱了广泛评估干预效果的能力。
科研与实践启示
未来的研究应优先考虑严格的研究设计,特别是随机对照试验,并具有明确的有效性定义和标准化的结果衡量标准。解决选择偏差并纳入长期结果评估对于提高证据质量至关重要。政策制定者和研究人员应采取示范性做法,例如参与性方法、多领域干预措施和结果三角测量,以提高“同一个健康”干预措施的可信度和适用性。

A scoping review of One Health interventions 2005–2025: What has been done, are they effective, and what are the best practices?

Authors: Eri Togami, Lincoln Hopkins, Lori Rosman, Meghan F. Davis
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: One Health research and practice are evolving rapidly, necessitating solid evidence-building and quality assessment of One Health interventions. We conducted a scoping review to (1) characterize how many and what kinds of One Health interventions are published in peer-reviewed literature; and (2) assess whether the identified interventions demonstrated effectiveness. We hypothesized that the evidence was limited in numbers and ability to demonstrate effectiveness. METHODS: The review was reported according to the Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses extension for Scoping Reviews. The initial search was conducted on Medline (Ovid), Embase, PubAg, and Web of Science in May 2024 and updated in December 2025. Inclusion criteria were: Primary research in which the investigators intervened with humans, animals, or their shared environment (at least two of three domains); an outcome assessment was included, and full text was available in English, published on or before December 16, 2025. Two researchers independently screened and analyzed all manuscripts. RESULTS: Over 18,000 references were screened, and 151 studies were included. Studies were conducted in 53 countries representing all six WHO Regions. The highest number of interventions were reported from the United States of America, Tanzania, and India. Among 79 interventions that targeted zoonoses, the most common diseases were general zoonoses, rabies, and cysticercosis. Over half (55%) of the study designs were quasi-experimental, followed by 23% randomized control trials (RCTs) and 19% non-randomized experiments. Less than half (41%) of the studies had a control or comparison group. The most common perceived sources of bias were immigrative (28%) and emigrative selection bias (11%). Eight field-based RCTs identified that a One Health intervention was effective in reducing disease occurrence or pathogen detection compared to the control. Exemplary practices included a comprehensive intervention spanning human, animal, and environmental domains, using a participatory approach, assessing longer-term outcomes, triangulating results using more than one method, not overstating findings, and providing a visual aid to describe the intervention. CONCLUSIONS: A small number of field-based RCTs clearly demonstrated the comparative effectiveness of One Health interventions, while it was challenging to assess the effectiveness of other studies without a more granular definition of effectiveness. Rigorous studies that assess the effectiveness of One Health interventions will collectively improve buy-in from governments, researchers, and policymakers, and advance the evidence base for employing the One Health approach.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines One Health interventions targeting zoonotic diseases, focusing on their effectiveness and best practices across human, animal, and environmental domains. The central question addresses the characterization of published interventions and their demonstrated effectiveness in peer-reviewed literature.
Evidence base and method
This scoping review followed PRISMA-ScR guidelines and searched Medline (Ovid), Embase, PubAg, and Web of Science in May 2024, updated in December 2025. Inclusion criteria required primary research with interventions spanning at least two of three One Health domains (human, animal, environment), outcome assessment, and full-text availability in English, published by December 16, 2025. Two researchers independently screened and analyzed manuscripts.
Consensus and conclusions
Eight field-based randomized controlled trials (RCTs) demonstrated that One Health interventions effectively reduced disease occurrence or pathogen detection compared to controls. The review highlights that a small but rigorous subset of studies provides clear evidence of effectiveness, while broader assessment is hindered by inconsistent definitions of effectiveness across studies.
Controversies and gaps
Less than half (41%) of studies included a control or comparison group, and over half (55%) used quasi-experimental designs, limiting causal inference. Selection bias was common, with immigrative (28%) and emigrative (11%) biases frequently perceived. The lack of a granular definition of effectiveness complicates cross-study comparisons and undermines the ability to assess intervention efficacy broadly.
Research and practice implications
Future research should prioritize rigorous study designs, particularly RCTs, with clear definitions of effectiveness and standardized outcome measures. Addressing selection bias and incorporating longer-term outcome assessments are critical for improving evidence quality. Policymakers and researchers should adopt exemplary practices, such as participatory approaches, multi-domain interventions, and triangulation of results, to enhance the credibility and applicability of One Health interventions.

🚨 突发动态与新闻 / Health News

Source: Texas Public Radio | TPR  |  Published: 2026-07-13
B 常规重要

州数据:贝克萨尔县是德克萨斯州确诊狂犬病病例最多的县 - 德克萨斯公共广播电台 |热塑性弹性体

State data: Bexar County tied for most confirmed rabies cases in Texas - Texas Public Radio | TPR
完整新闻中文摘要德克萨斯州公共广播电台报道称,贝克萨尔县与特拉维斯县并列 2026 年德克萨斯州狂犬病确诊病例数最多的县,报告了 17 至 33 例实验室确诊病例。州数据显示,大多数感染病例是蝙蝠,其次是臭鼬和浣熊,狐狸也受到严重影响。县官员将这一上升归因于加强的监控和之前的狐狸爆发。卫生当局敦促居民避开野生动物,给宠物接种疫苗,如果被咬或抓伤,请立即就医。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026年7月13日。活动数据期间:2026年1月1日至7月6日。
地点与对象
美国德克萨斯州贝克萨尔县。受影响人群:野生动物(蝙蝠、臭鼬、浣熊、狐狸、猫、狗、牛、山羊、马、郊狼)和贝克萨尔县居民。
事件
德克萨斯州公共广播电台援引德克萨斯州卫生服务部的监测数据报道,德克萨斯州贝克萨尔县确认爆发狂犬病。该县与特拉维斯县并列为该州确诊狂犬病病例数最多的县。
规模、影响与风险
2026 年,贝克萨尔县报告了 17 至 33 例实验室确诊的狂犬病病例。全州范围内,有 90 例确诊的蝙蝠病例、87 例臭鼬病例和 25 例浣熊病例。此次疫情涉及 20 只狐狸、14 只猫、2 头牛,以及狗、山羊、马和郊狼各 1 只。由于城市化带来的野生动物接触增加以及科马尔县沿线狐狸的严重爆发,人类面临的风险增加。
应对、状态与不确定性
州卫生官员已经发布了监测地图,并按病例范围对各县进行了分类。贝克萨尔县公共卫生主任安德里亚·格雷罗表示,这一增长的部分原因是意识和监测的加强。官员们敦促居民避开野生动物,给宠物接种疫苗,并立即报告咬伤或抓伤情况。如果无法找到或识别动物,建议采取暴露后预防 (PEP)。

San Antonio KTXI 90.1 FM - Hill Country KTPR 89.9 FM - Big Country KVHL 91.7 FM - Highland Lakes KTPD 89.3 FM - Del Rio KCTI 1450 AM - Gonzales Events TPR Events Community Calendar Cinema Tuesdays Great SA Summer Night City Think Science Worth Repeating Venue Rentals Illuminate and Inspire Gala TPR Events Community Calendar Cinema Tuesdays Great SA Summer Night City Think Science Worth Repeating Venue Rentals Illuminate and Inspire Gala Connect Contact Us TPR All Staff TPR News Staff TPR App TPR Newsletters How I Listen facebook instagram Contact Us TPR All Staff TPR News Staff TPR App TPR Newsletters How I Listen facebook instagram Support TPR Individual Membership Business Membership Business Directory Underwriting TPR Sponsorship Opportunities Legacy Vehicle Donation Real Estate Donation Volunteer With Texas Public Radio TPR Merchandise Workplace Giving Stocks, Securities, Other Endowment Individual Membership Business Membership Business Directory Underwriting TPR Sponsorship Opportunities Legacy Vehicle Donation Real Estate Donation Volunteer With Texas Public Radio TPR Merchandise Workplace Giving Stocks, Securities, Other Endowment My TPR Public Health State data: Bexar County tied for most confirmed rabies cases in Texas Texas Public Radio | By Kory Cook Published July 13, 2026 at 8:00 AM CDT Facebook Twitter LinkedIn Email Creative Touch Imaging Ltd / ReutersA raccoon Sign up for TPR Today, Texas Public Radio's newsletter that brings our top stories to your inbox each morning.According to surveillance maps, updated on July 6 and released this week by the Texas Department of State Health Services, both Bexar and Travis counties are in the state's highest case category, reporting between 17 and 33 laboratory-confirmed rabies cases in 2026. State officials group counties into ranges rather than releasing exact case totals, but Bexar County has previously confirmed at least 18 rabies cases this year.County officials have issued several public health advisories in recent months after infected foxes, skunks and other animals tested positive for the virus.Statewide, bats account for the highest number of laboratory-confirmed rabies cases this year, with 90 confirmed infections, followed by skunks with 87 cases and raccoons with 25. The state has also confirmed rabies in 20 foxes, 14 cats, two cows, and one each in a dog, goat, horse and coyote.Bexar County Public Health Director Andrea Guerrero said the increase in recorded cases is partly the result of heightened awareness and surveillance."Just being in the public consciousness has, I think, contributed to just having a lot more animals tested and a lot more positive cases," Guerrero said.She added that the county's high numbers may also be connected to a significant fox outbreak that occurred along the Comal County line last year."We had a pretty significant outbreak among foxes last year, where we had five positive foxes right on the Comal County line, and Comal County also had a higher number," Guerrero said. "Foxes and the rabies virus don't know geographic boundaries, and so we feel like that was just a population-based infection that was just kind of moving back and forth across the county lines."Guerrero said rabies remains endemic in much of Texas wildlife and that growing development has brought people and animals into closer contact."In terms of how rabies infection happens, it's endemic to most wildlife — and a lot of that wildlife population has become increasingly close to human populations with development and urbanization of previously rural areas," she said.Rabies is a viral disease that attacks the brain and central nervous system of warm-blooded animals. The virus is spread through the saliva of an infected animal, usually through bites or scratches. Once symptoms develop, rabies is almost always fatal.Health officials stress, however, that the disease is preventable if treatment begins quickly after a potential exposure.Guerrero said human deaths from rabies are rare because of prompt medical treatment and post-exposure vaccinations.County officials are urging residents to avoid approaching or handling wild animals, particularly those that appear sick, aggressive or unusually tame. They also recommend keeping pets vaccinated and supervising animals outdoors to prevent encounters with wildlife. Texas Department of Health and Human Services / Courtesy photoBexar County falls within the highest category for laboratory-confirmed rabies cases in Texas in 2026, according to state health officials. The county is reminding residents of a case from two years ago in which middle school students picked up a bat they found on a playground, illustrating how easily exposure can happen when people underestimate the risks posed by wild animals.Animals infected with rabies can display a variety of symptoms, including sudden changes in behavior, unexplained paralysis, difficulty swallowing, seizures, refusing food or appearing disoriented. Wild animals may also lose their natural fear of humans or become active at unusual times, such as skunks roaming during daylight hours.Officials caution that not every animal exhibiting these symptoms has rabies, but any animal acting abnormally should be avoided and reported to authorities.Anyone who is bitten, scratched or has broken skin after contact with an animal should immediately wash the wound with soap and water, seek medical attention and report the incident to Animal Care Services, the Bexar County Sheriff's Office or 911.Once a bite is reported, animal control officers investigate to determine whether the animal may have rabies and whether post-exposure prophylaxis, or PEP, is necessary. If the animal cannot be found or identified, health officials recommend consulting a doctor immediately about receiving preventive treatment. TPR was founded by and is supported by our community. If you value our commitment to the highest standards of responsible journalism and are able to do so, please consider making your gift of support today. View news five-element analysis
Time
Publication date: July 13, 2026. Event data period: January 1 to July 6, 2026.
Location and population
Bexar County, Texas, USA. Affected population: wildlife (bats, skunks, raccoons, foxes, cats, dogs, cows, goats, horses, coyotes) and residents of Bexar County.
Event
Confirmed rabies outbreak in Bexar County, Texas, reported by Texas Public Radio citing Texas Department of State Health Services surveillance data. The county is tied with Travis County for the highest number of confirmed rabies cases in the state.
Scale, impact and risk
Bexar County reported between 17 and 33 laboratory-confirmed rabies cases in 2026. Statewide, there are 90 confirmed bat cases, 87 skunk cases, and 25 raccoon cases. The outbreak includes 20 foxes, 14 cats, two cows, and one each in a dog, goat, horse, and coyote. The risk to humans is elevated due to increased wildlife contact from urbanization and a significant fox outbreak along the Comal County line.
Response, status and uncertainty
State health officials have released surveillance maps and categorized counties by case ranges. Bexar County Public Health Director Andrea Guerrero stated the increase is partly due to heightened awareness and surveillance. Officials are urging residents to avoid wild animals, keep pets vaccinated, and report bites or scratches immediately. Post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) is recommended if an animal cannot be found or identified.

Source: WOAH  |  Published: 2026-07-17
B 常规重要

在海狗身上发现的狂犬病凸显了狂犬病生态的前所未有的转变 - WOAH

Rabies detected in Cape fur seals highlights an unprecedented shift in rabies ecology - WOAH
完整新闻中文摘要2026 年 7 月 17 日,世界动物卫生组织报告称,在南部非洲的海狗中发现了持续的狂犬病传播。这一事件被描述为狂犬病生态学前所未有的转变,对野生动物管理和公共卫生提出了重大问题。该报告强调了“同一个健康”方法对于解决与这一发展相关的不断变化的风险的重要性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该新闻报道于 2026 年 7 月 17 日发布。所描述的事件是在海角海豹中检测到持续的狂犬病传播,据报道这是一个持续的发展。
地点与对象
该活动涉及南部非洲的海狗。受影响的种群是海豹种群,背景包括该地区更广泛的野生动物和人类种群。
事件
世界动物卫生组织 (WOAH) 报告称,在南部非洲的海狗中发现了狂犬病的持续传播。这被描述为狂犬病生态学前所未有的发展。
规模、影响与风险
该报告强调了狂犬病生态学前所未有的转变。它对野生动物管理和公共卫生提出了重要问题,表明疾病动态发生了重大且令人担忧的变化。
应对、状态与不确定性
报告认为该事件是一个需要关注的新事态发展。这意味着需要进一步调查,并强调“同一个健康”方法对于解决野生动物和人类健康风险的重要性。

Founded in 1924 as the Office International des Epizooties (OIE), in May 2003 we adopted the common name World Organisation for Animal Health. An intergovernmental organisation, we focus on transparently disseminating information on animal diseases, improving animal health globally and thus build a safer, healthier and more sustainable world Mission Advocacy Strategy Structure and GovernanceToggle submenu Back to menu World Assembly Council Director General Governance Review Committee Organisation’s Framework Members Collaborate with us What we doToggle submenu Back to menuWhat we doWe work to improve animal health and welfare across the globe. By collecting, analysing and disseminating veterinary scientific information, we encourage international solidarity in the control of animal health risks. Furthermore, we work across borders to foster a One Health approach, recognising that the health of animals, humans and the environment are interdependent. Nipah and Hendra virus diseases Working across borders to foster a One Health approach to global health. The health of animals and the environment strongly depend on human activities. See more One Health The “One Health” approach summarises a concept that has been known for more than a century; that human, animal and plant health are interdependent and bound to the health of the ecosystems in which they exist. We envisage and implement it as a collaborative, whole of society, whole of government approach to understanding, anticipating and addressing risks to global health. Discover Antimicrobial resistance Imagine a world where infections and diseases in humans, animals and plants are impossible to treat. This worst-case scenario could become a reality as bacteria, viruses and parasites develop resistance to the drugs we use to fight them. Antimicrobial resistance, or AMR, has become one of the most pressing health issues of our time. Solutions exist and everyone has a role to play in the fight against this global threat. Discover Disease Data Collection The World Animal Health Information System (WAHIS) is the global reference platform for the publication of official data on epidemiologically important diseases in domestic and wild animals. The data tracks the evolution of diseases, assists in trade and policy decisions, and informs research. Discover Animal Welfare Animal welfare is a complex and multi-faceted subject with scientific, ethical, economic, cultural, social, religious and political dimensions. It is attracting growing interest from civil society and is one of the priorities of the World Organisation for Animal Health (WOAH). Discover Aquatic animals Aquatic animals play a key role in achieving a more prosperous and secure world, contributing to Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs).
